# Seed 46 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - not small: hypervolume +6.4%, and no tail design compiled (Iteration 248)

`seed 46 tag2 10h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit bf966d97 (Iteration 241: the after-seed-45 batch - native
terminal output unbuffered, the SPN stages' -10 THROW, UTF-8 names in byte and
cell headers, the dispatch table as offsets, the gene jitos), started 02:26;
the evolution 02:29-10:39 (8 h 10 min - the owner's 10 hours - generations
0-186), the front measured again by 10:57, the comparison packed by 12:09.
46,498 designs made, 37,156 measured (36,705 alive), 9,342 screened - not
measured.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest -1.1%, smallest +0.15%,
memory +2 KB, **hypervolume +6.4%**, best Q -3.4% - **not small**: the stopping
rule counts afresh. The Q-best design of all runs is seed 43's cb9560d9dd: 0.194
at 26,608 bytes, 172 KB (Q 0.270 of s6's); the run's own Q-best fe3c9c3940, 0.188
at 25,696 bytes, 176 KB (Q 0.259 as the run measured it). Calibration 0.998 on
cpu 2, 0.999 on cpu 8; cpu 2 against cpu 8 median 1.004, Spearman 1.00.

From the run's database (Iteration 248):

- **Deaths: 451 - 229 "reach limit at scale 0 - not run" and 222 "engine did not
  compile": every design with the gene tail.** The dispatch table as offsets
  (Iteration 237) broke the tail engines: gen-tail.py reads the preprocessed
  source, where its guard turning the offsets off is already gone, so the
  handlers' table was differences between functions. Seed 46 ran without tail;
  fixed in the after-seed-46 batch (patch 4). None died on the corpus.
- The screen: 9,342 of the 44,957 children from generation 5 skipped (20.8%).
- Children by the try that made them: mutation 54.9%, crossover 33.7%, borrowed
  11.5% (the last fifth: crossover 36.9%).
- Via the attic: 7,126 children; 4 of the 127 on the final front.
- The final front, 127 designs: mutation 97, borrowed 18, crossover 9, carried 3.
- fsys: 2 on 54, 3 on 47, 5 on 17, 1 on 6, 4 on 3; the small third (15,872-
  16,767 bytes) all fsys 3 and threads 3; the fast third (23,799-30,549) all
  fsys 2.
- The JIT on 45 of the front (jitx and jitloop on all 45, jitinl on 38) -
  **jitos on none**: 33 designs with it lived, none reached the front.
- fbuf: 4 KB buffers on 66, 1 KB on 39, 512 bytes on 22.
- In the run: fastest 3bb2087f9d 0.172 at 30,549 (184 KB); smallest 3456ac1e5e
  15,872 (speed 1.410, 128 KB) - 424 bytes under seed 45's smallest (16,296): the
  dispatch offsets, less the UTF-8 names' bytes; least memory 4a7e5767d8, 124 KB.
- A try from the front repeats a design the run made 30.9% of the time by
  mutation alone, 1.0% by crossover.

---

# Evolved VM designs

37156 designs evaluated, 36705 alive, 9342 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): fe3c9c3940, speed 0.188, binary + image 25,696, memory 176 KB - Q 0.259 of s6-cv8b's.
